# Session Handoffs

Newest entry at TOP. Every session appends one entry before ending (use `/end-session`).

## Entry Template
```
## YYYY-MM-DD — <short title>
**Did:** what was completed (files touched, roadmap items checked)
**State:** does it run? tests green? any known breakage?
**Next:** the single next task, with any context the next session needs
**Watch out:** gotchas discovered this session
```

## 2026-11-11 (outline, buzz sizes, app version) -- Claude app session; Sonnet 5.5; Jeremy: "the zombie priority outline looks odd when they are made bigger from having subtasks. maybe we need to make the parent outline 2px" -> "i agree with you" -> "that looks better"; then "the phone buzz should be stronger ... as if your landing a big strong hit" -> "lets make massive anything 1hr or more. also give me dev tool buttons"; then "display the version in our settings window"; "wrap up"
**Did:** (1) **Priority outline on grown parents:** `css/enemyStatus.css` second `--outline-step` rule inside `@supports (width: round(up, 1.5px, 1px))`: `calc(round(up, var(--sprite-scale, 1), 1) * 1px / var(--sprite-scale, 1))` = 1px at scale <= 1, 2px for any parent with open sub-tasks; older browsers keep the 1px rule. ## 2026-11-03 (run-end carryover) -- A new run keeps open tasks, the best routine-slot count and (by flag) un-earnable items; the game-over card + How Deadline works explain it (Claude app session; Fable 5.1 decision -> Sonnet build; Jeremy)
**Did:** one task. Jeremy: players must not lose items or routine slots when a run ends, and the end screen must say what carries over. Recon found: Restart wiped held items, dropped slots to 1 (level resets, routines stay on), AND wiped typed-in one-off tasks (a bug against MECHANICS.md). Decisions (Jeremy): items = "anything not re-earnable by playing is kept until used" (a `keptAcrossRuns` flag on `CONFIG.SHOP_ITEMS`; NO row has it today because every item is re-earnable, so nothing is kept yet), carry open tasks, slots option A (never below the best ever). (3) script.js `startNextRun` replaces the Restart handler (carry tasks with ids + `itemIdCounter`, kept inventory, re-add via `addItemToGame`, fresh clock for overdue, rebuild parent rows, `sortAndRenderActiveList`); the game-over renderer passes `RunCarryover.summary`; dev Reset zeroes the best. (4) `GameOverView.buildCarryoverHtml` ("Next run": You keep / Starts over) + `css/gameOverReview.css`. (5) How Deadline works topic 7 `runs`. (6) index.html + sw.js tags (sw v240). Docs: NEW `docs/RUN_CARRYOVER_PLAN.md`, DECISIONS, MECHANICS, DATA_SCHEMA, ECONOMY, UI_UX, HOW_IT_WORKS_PLAN, ROADMAP, CLAUDE.md doc map.

Run it on the real folder to confirm 0 failures. Live in headless Chromium (844x390, service worker blocked): a hand-built dead level-5 save (3 slots, 2 kits + 1 token, 2 tasks + 1 sub-task, 1 overdue task, 1 routine task, 1 habit): the card showed "2 open tasks and 1 sub-task", "3 routine slots", "3 held items" ; Restart -> level 1, 100 HP, 0 points, empty inventory, `bestRoutineSlots` 3 and 3 slots, `itemIdCounter` 50, only the 3 typed tasks present (routine task + habit not carried), the overdue one at the church with a clock under 5 s old, both parent rows in the list; reload kept all of it; the `runs` topic opens expanded; no page errors. ## 2026-11-02 (late evening) -- Daily record: routine tally now accepts real (string) routine ids (Claude app session; Opus root cause -> Sonnet fix; Jeremy)
**Did:** the bug found during the points work. `DailyRecord.tally()` (js/dailyRecord.js) required a numeric routine id; real ids are strings (`routine_0_1730...`), so per-routine attendance (`rt` -> `att`) was never recorded in the real game. ## 2026-11-02 (evening) -- Week strip day buttons refresh on every task / habit / routine save (Claude app session; Opus root-cause -> Sonnet build; Jeremy)
**Did:** Jeremy: adding a task or habit for a day left that day's button number stale until a refresh. Root cause: `DayPagerView.refreshWeekStrip()` is reached only through script.js's shared `updateTaskCountDisplay()`, and the save paths had walked around it. (1) Five deps objects (`formsDeps`, `popupsDeps`, `habitInstanceDeps`, `routineTaskInstanceDeps`, the restore deps) carried an inline `updateTaskCountDisplay: () => Hud.updateTaskCountDisplay({...})` closure that only updated the HUD counter; Add Task and sub-task spawns went through those. They now pass the shared function. (2) These wrappers never recounted (a habit that is not due today spawns nothing, so no spawn-time refresh ran): `createHabitDefinition`, `createNewHabitInRoutine`, `createNewTaskInRoutine`, `editHabitInRoutine`, `editTaskInRoutine`, `removeHabitFromRoutine`, `removeTaskFromRoutine`, `transferHabitBetweenRoutines`, `transferTaskBetweenRoutines`, `addHabitToRoutine`, `toggleRoutineActive`, `deleteRoutine` -- each now calls `updateTaskCountDisplay()`. (3) `popupsDeps` and `routineViewsDeps` carry it, so the Edit Task save (js/ui/popups.js) and the routine picker's "Add Selected" (js/ui/routineViews.js, which duplicates `Routines.addHabitToRoutine` and changes membership itself) call `deps.updateTaskCountDisplay()` (optional dep). New `test/week-strip-refresh.test.js` (22): a source guard on script.js + view recount tests. sw v213.
**State:** green. Full Jest on a cloud clone of origin/main: 133 suites / 2,835. Cloud Chromium at 390x844 (real UI): task due in 3 days made that day's button 0 -> 1; a Saturday-only habit made Saturday 0 -> 1; a habit moved into an inactive routine took Tue-Sun 1 -> 0, routine ON -> 1, OFF -> 0, ON -> 1; the original code stayed at 0 through every step. Committed and pushed (origin 7eb467e); Jeremy phone-checked ("everything works").
**Next:** unchanged from the afternoon entry: (1) play a few days, then revisit hero healing with real data. (2) The parked timer-UI design session. (3) Nov 7 PixelLab reset: windmill T3, hospital tiers, re-measure `VILLAGE_ART_X`.
**Watch out:** (1) A new deps object that needs a count refresh must pass the shared `updateTaskCountDisplay`, never a new `Hud.updateTaskCountDisplay` closure: `test/week-strip-refresh.test.js` fails on a second direct call. (2) Any code that changes habit/task definitions, routine membership or a routine's active state must call `updateTaskCountDisplay()` afterward, because the strip counts later days from the DEFINITIONS (ghosts), not from spawned items. (3) Archiving a habit already refreshed the strip (2026-10-16); that path is unchanged. (4) Bridge: the device shell was down again; stage/commit worked, but one commit of script.js came back with the OLD size (the known stale write) and was fixed by committing again with the new mtime: always compare sizes. Docs are LF; script.js is CRLF.

**Did:** the whole pushback-charges system (plan `docs/PUSHBACK_CHARGES_PLAN.md`, DECISIONS 2026-11-02 x4). **A:** pure `js/pushback.js` + `CONFIG.PUSHBACK_SIZES / PUSHBACK_ALLOWANCE / PUSHBACK_PAID_ZONE_MS` (sw v205). **B:** `Pushback.quote / quoteFixed / apply`, `State.pushbackUses`, popup size buttons, edit-form live note, Base Health panel note, `item.pushedLate` + `done.late` (Clock tower skips pushed-late completions), DailyRecord **v6** `pb`, three `pushback_short/medium/long` shop tokens (held, 5/10/30), old pushback rows + `Economy.reschedulePrice` + `RESCHEDULE_*` removed (sw v206). **C:** `js/ui/pushbackHelp.js` (one copy builder), ⓘ in the popup / edit form / Shop header, charge dots on popup buttons, Shop "Pushback tokens" header (old "Tap a zombie" hint removed), Clock tower "What we measure" copy + "Your reward" block, queued star-up notice (`observeClockStar`, `FrozenNotice.showClockTowerStarUpNotice`) (sw v207). **Follow-up (Jeremy, phone check):** the 10-minute typo grace applies to tasks and sub-tasks only (`Pushback.graceCreatedAt`; habit and routine instances get none) (sw v208). ## 2026-10-30 (night) -- Village tiers: design DECIDED + Build 1 BUILT (Claude app session, Opus design -> Sonnet build)
**Did:** (1) Design, with Jeremy (all logged in DECISIONS 2026-10-30 night, plan in `docs/WORLD_FEEDBACK_PLAN.md` "Village tiers + building cards"): a building's tier = progress in the CURRENT run, only ever goes up, resets to T1 on game over (every new player/run starts with a T1 village); condition (overgrowth etc.) is a separate layer that CAN go down; blueprints REJECTED (runs must stay comparable); game-over card will show the village at its peak. One per-run counter per building: Radio tower = nights survived; Armory = tasks+sub-tasks+habits done; Clock tower = done minus late; Hospital = habit check-ins; Boxing gym = days with no slips (>= 1 bad habit resolved, none slipped); Jailhouse = nights the church ended >= 75% health; High school = perfect-attendance days (every active routine fully done, late counts; hero levels/stars/streak-tiers rejected as farmable); Windmill = heavy days handled (due > 1.25x the last-14-closed-days average AND >= 1 item above it, nothing missed, >= 3 days history). Thresholds T2/T3/T4/T5: nights 4/18/50/100, slain 30/150/450/1000, punctuality 25/120/350/750, habits 20/80/250/500, lurkers 3/14/40/80, defense 3/14/40/80, heroes 3/10/30/60, bestdays 2/5/15/30 (balance THEORY pass; re-check with real play). (2) Build 1 (Sonnet): `CONFIG.VILLAGE_TIER_THRESHOLDS` + `VILLAGE_DEFENSE_HELD_FRAC` / `VILLAGE_HEAVY_DAY_WINDOW|MIN_DAYS|FACTOR|MIN_EXTRA`; `DailyRecord` v2 (`att {active, perfect}` on closed days, working `rt` tally, `isPerfectAttendance`, `attendanceFromTally`; `Items` now sends `routineId` with complete/uncomplete/overdue/lurk-slipped via `dailyRoutineId`); pure `js/villageProgress.js` (`tierFor`, `counters`, `compute`, `extras`, `heavyDays`, `comebackDays`, `attendanceStreak`), wired into index.html + sw APP_SHELL, sw v144. Tests: `test/villageProgress.test.js`, `test/items-routine-attendance.test.js`. Docs: DATA_SCHEMA, DAILY_RECORD_PLAN, DECISIONS, ROADMAP, WORLD_FEEDBACK_PLAN, CLAUDE.md.

NOT wired into the scene: the village still reads the dev tiers (`?gymTier=` etc.), nothing calls `VillageProgress` yet. GitHub `main` = 08ecb5d (Build 1 + this HANDOFF/CLAUDE.md) and STILL LACKS the Windmill margin (> 1.25x the average AND >= 1 item above it). The first device write of the five margin files did not take (the bridge answered 'written' but the sizes on disk were unchanged), so Jeremy's `git commit` correctly said 'nothing to commit'. They were rewritten and size-verified at the end of the session (config.js 52,831 / villageProgress.js 11,086 / villageProgress.test.js 20,657 / DECISIONS.md 693,598 / ROADMAP.md 138,169 bytes). Jeremy must commit + push those five files; check `git log origin/main` for `VILLAGE_HEAVY_DAY_FACTOR` in js/config.js before starting Build 2.
**Next:** BUILD 2 (Sonnet, medium; plan in WORLD_FEEDBACK_PLAN "Build 2"): (1) script.js `get<Kind>Tier` read `VillageProgress.compute({runStats: State.getCurrentRunStats(), nightsSurvived: Damage.computeDaysSurvived(), days: Persistence.loadDays(), today: State.getToday(), runNumber: runHistory.length + 1, maxHp: CONFIG.MAX_BASE_HEALTH})`; keep the dev URL params as overrides; a new game must show a T1 village; read `deadline.days` once per day rollover / on change, not every tick. (2) Tappable buildings: `.village-layer` stays `pointer-events: none`, `.village-sprite` gets `pointer-events: auto` + cursor; zombies (z 30+) must stay above and keep their taps; transparent corners of the 128px canvas and back/far-row buildings tucked behind front ones need a hit-test (alpha or the `VILLAGE_ART_X` box). (3) `js/ui/villageCard.js` + `css/villageCard.css` via `Modal` (the opening click MUST `stopPropagation`): sprite at 2x, name, one-line "what it measures", T1-T5 pips, progress bar "N / M <unit> to reach T<n+1>" (`tierFor` gives prevAt/nextAt/toNext/fraction), "Fully rebuilt" at T5; school shows `extras.attendanceStreak`, windmill `extras.comebackDays`; empty states: gym "Add a habit to resist to start rebuilding", school "Start a routine to open the school". (b) The check-in answer for yesterday's bad habit lands in TODAY's record, so a gym day can credit a day late. (c) `routineId` comes from `findRoutineForItem`; frozen routines' tasks don't spawn and KO ghosts never go overdue, so neither can break attendance. (d) The Windmill needs >= 3 closed days before any day can be heavy, so no windmill progress before night 4. ## 2026-10-25 -- Design batch decided: KO ghosts, distance-priced rescheduling, work + limit timers (Claude app session, Fable; design only)
**Did:** Settled all three questions in `docs/DESIGN_QUESTIONS_KO_EDIT_TIMER.md` with Jeremy and rewrote that file as the spec + 4-session build plan. (1) KO ghosts = option C narrowed: a KO'd routine's items stay as faded, inert, COMPLETABLE ghosts — XP + habit success occurrence, 0 points, undone = excused (no miss/streak/damage); revive converts survivors with a clean-slate clock. (2) Rescheduling later is distance-priced: pushback tier for the distance × urgency-zone factor (>4 h free / 2-4 h 50% / <2 h 100% / overdue 100%), 10-min creation grace, 1 free paid-zone move per rolling week, Save blocked if unaffordable (never debt), `rescheduleCount` stats line; shop pushback unchanged; NO Edit token. (3) Timers: work timers on tasks + positive habits (hero plays Attack, base + routine damage × 0.5, "Still working?" at 60 min then auto-stop 10 min later, optional target auto-completes) and limit timers on negative habits (budget countdown; stop inside = Avoided; expiry → the existing "Time's up" arrival popup). Schema 14 planned (fields in DATA_SCHEMA.md). Added 7 `CONFIG` keys as DATA ONLY (`KO_GHOST_POINTS_FACTOR`, `RESCHEDULE_ZONE_FACTORS/GRACE_MS/FREE_PER_WEEK`, `WORK_TIMER_DAMAGE_FACTOR/CHECK_MS/AUTO_STOP_MS`), sw.js v88 -> v89. Docs: DECISIONS (new top entry), MECHANICS (new "Decided, NOT built" section + corrected the stale habit rate-bonus paragraph to config's 22/22/3×/2×), ECONOMY ("Rescheduling price"), ROUTINES (KO note), DATA_SCHEMA (planned v14), UI_UX, ROADMAP (design item checked, 4 build items added), CLAUDE.md, balance-tuning SKILL.md canonical list.
**State:** No behaviour change. Jest 78 suites / 1,637 green, run in a cloud clone of origin/main (`f843b3f`, which matches local main) + the same config insert and sw bump; the device shell's npm is still blocked (403). Everything uncommitted. Not live-checked (nothing to see).
**Next:** Build 1 — KO ghosts + schema 14 (include the revive-time "When is this due now?" picker for stale one-off task/sub-task ghosts — Jeremy said yes, free, dismiss = keep original; reuse `Popups.promptNewDueTime`) (ROADMAP M5 sub-item; spec section Q1 + "Schema 14" + "Build order"). Sonnet, medium; switch up to Opus only if the loop/overdue/catch-up guards get tangled. Then builds 2-4 in order.
**Watch out:** (1) `clearActiveInstancesForRoutine` is shared by KO, manual deactivation and frozen slots — only the KO path stops recalling; the other two keep removing. (2) Ghosts must be skipped by `markAsOverdue`, the loop's damage tick, BOTH catch-up paths, `selectHabitDefsToSpawn` (don't double-spawn a ghosted habit), and the rollover "positive habit → dropped" branch must record NO miss for a ghost. (3) `KO_GHOST_POINTS_FACTOR` multiplies points only — XP and `occurrenceHistory` stay normal, so `RunStats` counters still record the completion. (4) Reschedule price is computed against the CURRENT due at save time (not the original) — that is what defeats move-earlier-then-later. (5) Timer elapsed = now − `startedAt` + `timerMsBanked`; never count loop ticks. (6) Starting a limit timer must set `arrivalPromptedFor` so the time-of-day prompt doesn't also fire.

## 2026-10-13 (night wrap-up) -- Lane z-order + lane switching, sprite feet calibration, strip back lane lowered (Claude app session, Opus plan -> Sonnet build)
**Did:** Jeremy's phone check found a middle-lane zombie drawn behind a front-lane overdue one, then a back-lane nurse floating above the floor. (1) `ListOrder.zIndexes(layout, items, { lanes })`: z follows the lane (front 62-77, middle 46-61, back 30-45; `LIST_Z_LANE_STRIDE` 16), list order only ranks inside a lane; `Layout.applyStripLayout` / `applyGroundLanes` write `--list-z` + `data-lane` every tick, `ListOrder.applyZ` reads `data-lane` on list render. (2) `Layout.assignLanes`: zombies closer than `CONFIG.LANE_CONFLICT_FRAC` (0.6) x sprite width move to the nearest free lane (strip AND landscape); overdue attackers at the base now use lanes (CSS `bottom: 4px + (2 - lane) x pitch`) and the strip's "+N" appears only when all 3 lanes are blocked; fixed `z-index: 80/79` on the pile/lurker replaced by `--list-z`. (3) Feet: measured every atlas (lowest opaque row; walk = max, idle/attack = median) and added per-category/state `--dy-walk/--dy-idle/--dy-attack` in `css/enemyAnim.css` (other 7/10/10, career 3/2/3, creativity 0/2/2, spirituality 2/2/2, health -2/0/-4, lifestyle -2/0/0); `test/spriteFeet.test.js` re-measures with pngjs. (4) Strip geometry: `STRIP_FLOOR_FEET_INSET` 8, sky 24 -> 32, pitch 14 -> 10 (canvas still 92px) so the back lane stands in the dirt (the tile's ground starts ~6 rows down). (5) Landscape overflow fan-out: the 4th+ zombie overlapping three others is offset right by 24px x rank (`Layout.computeGroundLayout` offsets, `--lane-dx` margin in `css/enemySprites.css`, `CONFIG.LANE_OVERFLOW_OFFSET_PX`) -- Jeremy's pick over a badge. `sw.js` v44 -> v47. ## 2026-07-21 — Zombies stand on the V1 ground band (movement.js follow-up, Cowork session, Opus)

**Did:** Closed the V1 follow-up ("put zombies on the ground band") split off during the parallax session. `Movement.getItemTopPosition` no longer randomizes a top-level item across the full canvas height — it now lands the item's FEET (sprite box bottom, same line sub-tasks bottom-align to) at a random fraction of canvas height between `CONFIG.GROUND_BAND_FEET_TOP_FRAC` (0.85, ≈ fence line) and `CONFIG.GROUND_BAND_FEET_BOTTOM_FRAC` (0.99, ≈ canvas floor). Two new tunable layout constants in `js/config.js`. Sub-task bottom-alignment untouched. Files: `js/config.js`, `js/movement.js`, `test/movement.test.js` (two full-canvas assertions rewritten to the band + a new full-jitter-range bounds test), `docs/UI_UX.md`, `docs/MECHANICS.md`, `docs/ROADMAP.md` (checked off), `docs/DECISIONS.md`. Commit pushed by Jeremy.

**Follow-up commit — `sw.js` CACHE_NAME bumped v2→v3 (same session, after the first push):** `js/config.js` and `js/movement.js` are both cache-first PWA shell assets in `sw.js`'s `APP_SHELL`, so an already-installed phone/PWA would have served the STALE cached copies and never shown the ground-band change until the version string changed. Caught while answering Jeremy's "how do I push this to my phone" — bumped per sw.js's own "BUMP CACHE_NAME on any shell-file change" rule. Needs its own `git add -A && git commit && git push` (Jeremy's first push predated this bump). Phone-deploy path confirmed: GitHub Pages (repo github.com/schooley02/Deadline, likely `https://schooley02.github.io/Deadline/`) → open in phone Chrome → Add to Home Screen; SW activates the new cache on the SECOND load after an update (first load serves old cache while fetching new in background), so the change may take one extra app-open to appear.

**The overlap judgment call (what the split was for):** narrowing the range clusters items vertically, risking same-timeline enemies (identical due time → identical x) stacking exactly. The ~56px jitter (14% of a 401px canvas) resolves it — same-x items land at staggered feet heights. Live-verified: two 11:59 PM tasks spawned at x=655 with feet at frac 0.893 and 0.961 (both in-band, 27px apart); a 2,000-sample sweep landed every foot in [0.85, 0.99]. Judged acceptable — a real ground-line crowd overlaps somewhat anyway, and exact-same-time collisions are the uncommon worst case.

**State:** 60 suites, 1271/1271 (+1). **Jest ran clean from `$HOME` this session** — the prior session's sandbox jest wall did NOT recur (try it normally next session; if it breaks again, `node --check` + live Chrome is the fallback). Live-verified in Chrome against Jeremy's real `node server.js`: single restored zombie grounded at feet frac 0.961; two-zombie same-x stagger confirmed by measurement + screenshot; zero console errors. Jeremy's real save left untouched (1 task, XP/Points 10, Health 100) — the two-zombie test task was removed cleanly afterward (see Watch out).

**Next:** Per the V1 handoff's standing queue — V2 (state-indicator glow redesign) → V3b (portrait glance strip) → V3c (landscape scene + tap-to-interact); V4 (AI-generate pixel church) anytime; V5 (design tokens) anytime; V6 (juice pass) waits on Jeremy's play week. Also still open, unscheduled: the `uncompleteItem` overdue back-charge sibling hazard (needs Jeremy's design call, ROADMAP Known bugs) and re-checking the shop's "days to afford X" prose against real play data.

**Watch out:**
- **The ground-band fractions (0.85/0.99) are tuned against the V1 scene's CSS `.scene-ground` (bottom 24%, fence near its top).** If the ground band's height or the fence position in `css/gameCanvas.css` ever changes, re-check these two `CONFIG` values — they're matched to the CSS by hand (deliberately NOT coupled to the DOM rect; `getItemTopPosition` only receives `canvasHeight`). They're tunable and layout-only, not economy balance.
- **Persistence/saveGame/State are all closure-scoped — NOT on `window`.** The documented "stub `Persistence.flush`/`requestSave` before a direct localStorage edit" trick silently no-ops here (the `if (window.Persistence)` guard is false). To hand-edit `localStorage` and survive a reload, block the write at the source instead: override `localStorage.setItem` to drop writes to the `deadline.save` key, THEN navigate — the unload-flush's `setItem` gets swallowed and your edit persists. (First reload attempt this session failed exactly because the stub missed and the unload-flush re-serialized live state over the edit.)
- Effects are currently set to **Off** in Jeremy's settings (`fx-off` body class) — that's why idle cloud drift/fog/walk-bob don't animate. Not a bug. Toggle via Settings → "Streak Fire Effects" → Full/Reduced.
- Same SW-unregister + Cache-Storage-clear-then-navigate dance was needed to pick up the `movement.js`/`config.js` edits against the live dev server — a plain reload didn't.

## 2026-07-21 — V1 shipped: graveyard atmosphere + slider-coupled parallax (Cowork session, Sonnet)

**Did:** Closed VISUAL_DESIGN_PLAN.md's V1 item, next in the standing visual-track queue (fully spec'd by the earlier 2026-07-21 art-direction Fable session). New `.graveyard-scene` layer stack behind `#base`/`#heroBaseZone`/`#midnightLine`/`.enemy` in `#gameCanvas` (`css/gameCanvas.css`, `index.html`): night-sky gradient + static moon glow, faint clouds, a far hill + 3 bare-tree silhouettes and a fence + 3 tombstones (both inline SVG, no new binary asset needed), midnight line restyled from the old red alarm hairline to a dark iron gate-post with a lantern glow. Slider-coupled parallax: `js/ui/timeSliderView.js` sets a new `--scene-progress` CSS var (0–1, minutes-of-day) on every live tick AND scrub, read by `.scene-clouds`/`.scene-silhouettes`'s `translateX(calc(...))` at different rates (clouds fastest, silhouettes slower); `.scene-ground` deliberately stays untransformed (fixed, per the art-direction decision — the stillness against the moving layers is what sells the depth). Idle cloud drift + ground fog are separate keyframe animations gated behind `fx-off`/`fx-reduced` + `prefers-reduced-motion`, matching the existing streak-fire/walk-bob pattern. `sw.js` CACHE_NAME bumped v1→v2 (index.html/gameCanvas.css/timeSliderView.js all changed; no new cacheable files since props are inline SVG). Updated `docs/UI_UX.md` (new section), `docs/VISUAL_DESIGN_PLAN.md` (checked off), `docs/ROADMAP.md`, `docs/DECISIONS.md`.

**Found + fixed live in Chrome (not caught by static review):** the scene was completely invisible on first load despite every layer having correct computed styles/rects — `#gameCanvas` needed an explicit `z-index: 0` to establish its own stacking context, otherwise the scene's negative-z-index layers (intentionally negative so no OTHER element's z-index had to change) escaped upward past `#gameCanvas` entirely instead of staying scoped behind its children. One-line fix; logged in UI_UX.md as a general gotcha for any future negative-z decorative layer.

**Deliberately NOT done — split per the task's own stop condition:** "zombies bottom-aligned to the ground band" only shipped as a visual band; zombies don't actually stand on it yet. Checked before writing CSS: `Movement.getItemTopPosition` randomizes a top-level item's vertical position across the FULL canvas height, not a bottom band — narrowing that range is real lane-math (affects multi-item overlap), which is exactly the "STOP and split, that's movement.js" condition the V1 task itself called out. Logged as its own ROADMAP follow-up rather than decided unilaterally in a CSS-scoped session.

**Live-tuning pass (same session, several rounds of Jeremy's direct feedback in Chrome — all `css/gameCanvas.css` + one small `js/ui/timeSliderView.js` addition):**
1. Clouds brightened + parallax rates widened (clouds -9%→-18%, silhouettes -3%→-7%) so the slider-driven motion actually reads.
2. **Ground now moves too, and fastest of all layers** (was intentionally fixed per the original art-direction call) — Jeremy's observation: zombies stand on the ground band and visibly move as you scrub, so a perfectly static ground under them read as sliding rather than walking. This also happens to match REAL parallax physics (closest layer moves most) rather than the original stylized inversion. Rate: `-22%` (steeper than clouds' `-18%`), with `.scene-ground` widened to `130%/-15%` (matching the clouds' margin trick) so the bigger swing never reveals a gap at the canvas edge. **This reverses the "ground fixed" decision logged earlier today — if it holds up, treat that DECISIONS.md entry as superseded, not the current state.**
3. Idle cloud "wind drift" added, tuned across 4 rounds: brighter → sped up 70s→12s → rebuilt from a `transform`-based wiggle (bounded, snapped back visibly) into a seamless `background-position` loop on a repeating tile (sails off the right edge, reappears on the left, no pop) → tile widened 500px→1100px with fewer shapes (4→2) per Jeremy's "too many clouds" call, with duration scaled 7s→15s in the same step to hold the px/sec speed constant through the density change. `js/ui/timeSliderView.js`'s `handleScrubInput`/`handleRelease` now toggle a `.parallax-scrubbing` class on `#graveyardScene` so the idle drift PAUSES for the duration of an active scrub (`animation-play-state: paused`) and resumes on release — keeps the idle "wind" and the slider-driven parallax from ever visually fighting each other.

**State:** `node --check` clean on `js/ui/timeSliderView.js`; module load-verified via a direct `require()` (no syntax/runtime error). **`npx jest` could not be run this session** — a new sandbox-environment finding, unrelated to this session's code: even a from-scratch minimal `jest.config.js` + trivial test in a brand-new sandbox directory fails jest's own config-validation resolver for a `setupFilesAfterEnv` file that `fs.existsSync`/`require()`/`graceful-fs` all independently confirm exists and is readable. Reproduced outside this repo entirely, so it reads as sandbox infrastructure, not a regression — but it means today's verification leaned entirely on `node --check` + live Chrome rather than the usual green-suite number. Flag if a future session hits the same wall. Live-verified end-to-end in Chrome against Jeremy's real `node server.js`: desktop scene renders correctly (hill/trees/fence/tombstones/moon all visible, matches the "playful, slightly eerie, never grim" direction); 390×844 same-origin iframe showed zero page-level overflow; `--scene-progress`/`translateX` swept correctly across the full 0–1439 minute range via direct slider-value testing (ground stayed untransformed throughout, confirming "ground fixed"); `fx-off`/`fx-reduced` both correctly stopped the idle animations, default left them running; hero-chip zone (base's leftmost 120px) unchanged; zero console errors beyond the standard Chrome-extension message-channel noise.

## 2026-07-21 — V3a shipped: agenda rows no longer clip at 390px; Reset Game gated to dev mode (Cowork session, Sonnet)

**Did:** Closed VISUAL_DESIGN_PLAN.md's V3a item, next in the standing visual-track queue. (1) Agenda-row clipping at 390px: fixed in two separate passes after live measurement showed the first pass wasn't actually clean — `.item-info`'s internal title/details rows (unclassed JS-built divs, targeted structurally via `.item-info div { flex-wrap: wrap }` in `css/responsive.css`, plus `min-width:0`/`overflow-wrap:break-word` on `.item-name` in `css/agendaList.css`, plus `flex-shrink:1 !important` on `.task-controls` to beat its inline `flex-shrink:0`), then a SECOND chain found only via `getBoundingClientRect` measurement: sub-tasks render as a sibling of `.item-info` (not a descendant), so their own unwrapped row (`.sub-task-item`/`.sub-task-info`/`.sub-task-controls` — classed, no structural selector needed) was still dragging the whole card wider. (2) Reset Game (moved into Settings earlier today, see the entry below) was unconditionally visible to every player on the live build — new pure `SettingsView.isDevMode(loc)` gates it to `localhost`/`127.0.0.1` or a `?dev` query param; exported + unit-tested (`test/settings-view-devmode.test.js`, +6). Updated `docs/UI_UX.md`, `docs/VISUAL_DESIGN_PLAN.md` (checked off), `docs/DECISIONS.md`.

Live-verified in Chrome against Jeremy's real `node server.js`: a same-origin 390×844 iframe (standing mobile-verification recipe) showed a real task + real sub-task rendering with zero clipping, confirmed quantitatively via `scrollWidth`/`clientWidth`/`getBoundingClientRect` on the row/container/body/html (zero overflow anywhere except `.game-canvas`, which is pre-existing/out-of-scope, queued separately under V1/V3b). Settings → Reset Game correctly shown on localhost; `isDevMode` correctly computed `false` for a simulated production hostname with no `?dev` param; Reset itself still works end-to-end. **Next:** V1 (parallax graveyard) → V2 (state-indicator glow redesign) → V3b (portrait glance strip) → V3c (landscape scene + tap-to-interact); V4 (AI-generate pixel church) anytime — art source already unblocked (2026-07-21 art-direction session); V5 (design tokens) anytime; V6 (juice pass) waits on Jeremy's play week. **Watch out:**
- The `.item-info div` / `.sub-task-item` flex-wrap fixes are BOTH scoped inside `css/responsive.css`'s existing `max-width:768px` block — desktop is untouched by design. If a future session adds new unclassed flex rows inside `.item-info` (or a new sibling section next to it, like `.sub-tasks-section`), they will NOT automatically inherit this fix — check for the same nowrap-flex-row overflow pattern before assuming CSS-only rows are safe at mobile widths.
- A "looks clean in a screenshot" check is NOT sufficient for this class of bug — the first pass here visually looked fine but still measured 49px of real overflow. Use `el.scrollWidth > el.clientWidth` (or `getBoundingClientRect` comparisons against the parent) as the actual pass/fail signal, not just eyeballing a screenshot.
- `isDevMode`'s `?dev` escape hatch works on ANY origin including the live GitHub Pages build — intentional (Jeremy can still spot-check Reset there), but means Reset is technically reachable by anyone who knows to append `?dev` to the URL. Acceptable per V3a's scope (this is dev chrome, not a security boundary); flag if that assumption ever needs to change.

## 2026-07-21 — Overdue-edit back-charge bug fixed: editing a due date into the past no longer replays missed damage intervals (Cowork session, root cause + design resolved 2026-07-20, Sonnet execution)

**Did:** Closed the last unguarded entry of the 2026-07-18 far-past-clock bug family (ROADMAP Known bugs). Root cause: `Items.markAsOverdue(item, currentTime, deps)` has always ignored its own `currentTime` parameter — it unconditionally parks `item.lastDamageTickTime = item.dueDateTime.getTime()`. `spawning.js`'s created-already-overdue path already worked around this with a one-line override (`itemData.lastDamageTickTime = Date.now()`) right after calling `markAsOverdue`; `recomputeOverdueStateAfterEdit`'s pulled-into-the-past branch (fires when an edit moves a due date backward) never got the same treatment, so a same-day 10PM→9AM edit dealt −92 HP in seconds. Fix mirrors spawning.js's exact pattern: `item.lastDamageTickTime = now.getTime()` immediately after `markAsOverdue(item, now, deps)` in that branch. Both design forks were already resolved 2026-07-20 (Jeremy, Fable) — this was pure Sonnet execution. New `test/overdue-edit.test.js` (6 tests). Updated `docs/ROADMAP.md` (checked off), `docs/MECHANICS.md` (new Overdue Damage bullet), `docs/DECISIONS.md`.

**Found, not fixed:** `uncompleteItem` (items.js:1043) has the identical latent pattern when undoing a completion on a past-due item — logged as a new unchecked ROADMAP Known bugs entry rather than silently applying the same fix, since "undo a completion you knew was overdue" may deserve different treatment than an edit and wasn't part of the original bug report. Needs Jeremy's call before touching.

**State:** 59 suites, 1264/1264 (+6). `node --check` clean on items.js. Live-verified in Chrome against Jeremy's real `node server.js`: created a task due in the future, edited its due time to ~12 hours in the past, confirmed Health stayed at 100 on landing (previously would have dropped 30+ HP), and confirmed via `State.getActiveItems()`/`State.getBaseHealth()` in-page that `lastDamageTickTime` sat ~13 seconds in the past (the edit moment) while the due date itself was ~12 hours back — the clock demonstrably did NOT get backdated to the due time. Zero console errors. Dev save reset to pristine afterward via the in-app Settings → Reset Game flow.

**Next:** Unchanged from prior entries — V3a (agenda rows clip at 390px) → V1 (parallax) → V2 → V3b → V3c next on the visual track; V4 (AI-generate pixel church) anytime; V6 waits on the play week. The newly-found `uncompleteItem` sibling hazard is unscheduled — needs a design decision from Jeremy before it becomes a ticket. Also still open from two sessions ago: re-check the shop's session-24 balance rationale against real play data once Jeremy has some — every "days to afford X" figure is now ~10x larger post the points re-tune.

**Watch out:**
- `markAsOverdue`'s `currentTime` parameter is STILL a dead parameter at its other 3 call sites (loop.js, damage.js, items.js:1043's uncompleteItem) — only the edit path got the override this session, deliberately scoped narrow (see DECISIONS.md's "alternatives considered"). ## 2026-07-21 — Points economy re-tuned 10x down; sub-tasked parents pay 0 pts of their own; habit rate bonus re-anchored to a 22-occurrence window (Cowork session, Jeremy's call throughout, Sonnet execution)

**Did:** Balance-tuning protocol session, Jeremy's request. `CONFIG.POINTS_PER_TASK`/`POINTS_PER_HABIT`/`SUBTASK_POINTS` 10/5/5 → 1/1/1 — shop prices (25-300 pts) deliberately left unchanged so tokens (freezes, pushbacks, cheat days) cost real sustained effort instead of a day's grinding; XP untouched (still 10/5), so leveling pace doesn't move. New rule in `Items.completeItem`/`uncompleteItem`: a parent task that ever had a sub-task (`completedSubTasks > 0`) now earns 0 points of its own on final completion — all the points for a sub-tasked task come from completing its subs, not from checking off the parent (XP unaffected). Habit rate bonus re-anchored off the old "task parity" model (meaningless once tasks are worth 1) to Jeremy's "22 reps make a habit" framing: `HABIT_RATE_WINDOW`/`HABIT_RATE_MIN_SAMPLE` 14/7 → 22/22 (no tier fires until a full 22-occurrence history exists), `HABIT_RATE_TIERS` 90%/70%→2×/1.5× → 100%/80%→3×/2×. All three design calls (sub-task rounding/behavior, habit tier redesign, min-sample gating) were Jeremy's explicit choices via clarifying questions before implementation — see DECISIONS.md for the full rationale and rejected alternatives. Updated `js/config.js`, `js/items.js`, `docs/ECONOMY.md`, `docs/DECISIONS.md`, `docs/ROADMAP.md`, and 3 test files whose expectations were pinned to the old numbers/behavior (`test/subtask-economy.test.js` rewritten with 2 new tests, `test/habits.test.js`'s tier tests rebuilt around the 22-window, `test/items-indulge.test.js`'s one fixture balance adjusted). Jeremy also hand-updated `.claude/skills/balance-tuning/SKILL.md`'s canonical values list himself (protected from Cowork edits) after one round of feedback on a merge slip.

**State:** 58 suites, 1258/1258 (+2) in the sandbox. `node --check` clean on config.js/items.js. Live-verified end-to-end in Chrome against Jeremy's real `node server.js`: a standalone task paid exactly 10 XP/1 pt; a sub-task paid 5 XP/1 pt and then completing its parent paid +10 XP/+0 pts (points-per-item math confirmed live, matching the new rule exactly); a brand-new habit (below the 22-sample minimum) paid the flat 5 XP/1 pt base. Zero console errors throughout. Dev save reset to pristine via the in-app Settings → Reset Game flow (built last session) afterward.

**Next:** Unchanged from the prior entries — overdue-edit bugfix first (small, fully specified in ROADMAP Known bugs), then V3a → V1 (parallax) → V2 → V3b → V3c; V4 (AI-generate pixel church) anytime; V6 waits on the play week. Separately, worth flagging for a future session: the shop's session-24 balance rationale (HP-per-point tiers, break-even math) still holds in RATIO terms but every "days to afford X" figure in ECONOMY.md's prose is now ~10x larger — re-check against real play data once Jeremy has some, same standing follow-up as before, just a bigger gap now.

**Watch out:**
- The habit rate bonus now requires a FULL 22 recorded occurrences before any multiplier can fire (both the 2× and 3× tiers) — a habit started today won't see its first possible bonus for 22 scheduled occurrences (up to 22 days out for a daily habit). This is intentional (Jeremy's "22 reps" framing) but will look like "the bonus is broken" to anyone who doesn't know the design — flag it if it comes up in a future bug report.
- `.claude/skills/balance-tuning/SKILL.md` is NOT editable from the Cowork sandbox (Edit tool blocks it as a protected path) — any future canonical-values update from a Cowork session needs to be handed to Jeremy as exact text to paste himself, same as this session.
- Achievement thresholds, the debt/break-even HUD nudge, and shop prices were deliberately NOT touched — the break-even nudge already derives its "complete N tasks" figure from `pointsPerTask` dynamically so it reads correctly at any value with no code change, it'll just show bigger N now.
